Now, maybe `np.correlate(h, 1-h, 'full')` for continuous h uses convolution where output first half is not exactly p_r? We assumed. Let's verify with a non-symmetric small example using manual formula from numpy docs. If mismatch, our optimization objective differs from evaluator. Need be absolutely sure. Numpy correlate: `c_k = sum_n a_{n+k} * v_n`. Full output index `k + len(v) -1`. So first half (indices 0 to n-1) corresponds k=-(n-1) to 0. For index idx, k=idx-(n-1). For idx=n-1-r => k=-r. c = ∑_{n} a_{n-r} v_n. Let i=n-r => n=i+r. Sum i=0..n-1-r a_i v_{i+r} = ∑ h_i(1-h_{i+r}) = p_r. Yes. Output order idx 0 r=n-1, idx n-1 r=0. So first half is p_{n-1} ... p0. We used c[:n]=p[::-1]. Good. Positive half q_r = ∑ a_{n+r} v_n = h_{n+r}(1-h_n) = q_r? Our q_r defined ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-r} h_{i+r}(1-h_i); yes. Output index n+r? idx=n-1+r => k=r; c = ∑_{n=0}^{n-1-r} h_{n+r} (1-h_n) = q_r. We used c[n:]=q[1:] (index n is r=1). Good. Center idx n-1 k=0 included in p0. q0 duplicate not. Good.

Gradients for p/q match derivatives of these exact output entries. Great.

Now, if there is a mismatch in `compute_pq` A alignment: A_r = ∑ h_i h_{i+r}. For r=0 sum h^2. Direct `aut=np.correlate(h,h,'full')`. For h=[1,2,3]? aut? A0=14; A1=1*2+2*3=8; A2=1*3=3. np.correlate([1,2,3],[1,2,3]) output? I think [3,8,14,8,3]? index 0 A2=3, 1 A1=8,2 A0=14, yes. aut[:n]=[3,8,14], reversed [14,8,3]. Good. FFT ac[0]=14, ac[1]=8, ac[2]=3. Good. P_r = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-r} h_i. For r1 P=1+2=3; p1=P-A=3-8? Wait h=[1,2,3] not bounded [0,1], p1 = h0(1-h1)+h1(1-h2) =1*(-1)+2*(-2)=-5. Formula P-A=3-8=-5. For values [0,1], nonnegative. Good. q1=(total - S1)-A? total6, S1=1 =>5-8=-3; q1 = h1(1-h0)+h2(1-h1)=2*0+3*(-1)=-3. Good.

For binary, p nonnegative. Good.

Now, final answer. Need no analysis. Let's produce code. Ensure final fence closed.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import random
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def enforce_sum(h, target):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    target = float(target)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
        return h
    if diff > 0:
        idx = np.where(h < 1.0)[0]
        if idx.size == 0:
            return h
        order = idx[np.argsort(1.0 - h[idx])]
        for k in order:
            adj = min(1.0 - h[k], diff)
            if adj <= 1e-15:
                continue
            h[k] += adj
            diff -= adj
            if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
                break
    else:
        idx = np.where(h > 0.0)[0]
        if idx.size == 0:
            return h
        order = idx[np.argsort(h[idx])]
        for k in order:
            adj = min(h[k], -diff)
            if adj <= 1e-15:
                continue
            h[k] -= adj
            diff += adj
            if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
                break
    return h


def project_feasible(h, target):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    target = float(target)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(200):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            s = room.sum()
            if s < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / s) * room
        else:
            s = h.sum()
            if s < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / s) * h
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    h = enforce_sum(h, target)
    return h


def resample_h(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if len(h) == n:
        return h.copy()
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(h), endpoint=False)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    return np.interp(x1, x0, h)


def binarize(h, target):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    target = float(target)
    hb = (h >= 0.5).astype(float)
    diff = target - hb.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-10:
        if diff > 0:
            zeros = np.where(hb < 0.5)[0]
            if zeros.size:
                order = zeros[np.argsort(-h[zeros])]
                k = int(min(np.ceil(diff), zeros.size))
                for idx in order[:k]:
                    hb[idx] = 1.0
        else:
            ones = np.where(hb > 0.5)[0]
            if ones.size:
                order = ones[np.argsort(h[ones])]
                k = int(min(np.ceil(-diff), ones.size))
                for idx in order[:k]:
                    hb[idx] = 0.0
    diff = target - hb.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-10:
        if diff > 0:
            z = np.where(hb < 0.5)[0]
            if z.size:
                hb[z[0]] = min(1.0, hb[z[0]] + diff)
        else:
            o = np.where(hb > 0.5)[0]
            if o.size:
                hb[o[0]] = max(0.0, hb[o[0]] + diff)
    return hb


def fast_conv(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float)
    la, lb = a.size, b.size
    if la == 0 or lb == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    nconv = la + lb - 1
    if la * lb <= 200000:
        return np.convolve(a, b, mode="full")
    m = 1
    while m < nconv:
        m <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, m)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, m).real
    return out[:nconv]


def compute_pq(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float), np.array([], dtype=float)
    if n == 1:
        p = np.array([h[0] * (1.0 - h[0])], dtype=float)
        q = p.copy()
        return p, q
    if n <= 64:
        aut = np.correlate(h, h, mode="full")
        A = aut[:n][::-1].astype(float)
    else:
        m = 1
        while m < 2 * n - 1:
            m <<= 1
        f = np.fft.rfft(h, m)
        ac = np.fft.irfft(f * np.conj(f), m)[:2 * n - 1]
        A = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
        A[0] = ac[0]
        A[1:] = ac[1:n]
    S = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(h)))
    r = np.arange(n)
    P = S[n - r]
    Qbase = S[-1] - S[:n]
    p = P - A
    q = Qbase - A
    p0 = float(h.sum()) - float(np.dot(h, h))
    p[0] = p0
    q[0] = p0
    return p, q


def compute_c5_count(h, n):
    p, q = compute_pq(h)
    return float(max(p.max(), q.max()))


def compute_c5_exact(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if n <= 0:
        return 0.0
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(c.max() * (2.0 / n))


def soft_max_weights(c, alpha):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return m, np.zeros_like(c)
    if alpha <= 0.0:
        w = (c >= m - 1e-14).astype(float)
        s = w.sum()
        if s > 0:
            w /= s
        else:
            w[0] = 1.0
        return m, w
    u = alpha * (c - m)
    u -= u.max()
    w = np.exp(u)
    s = w.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        w = (c >= m - 1e-14).astype(float)
        s2 = w.sum()
        if s2 > 0:
            w /= s2
        else:
            w[0] = 1.0
        return m, w
    w /= s
    val = m + np.log(s) / alpha
    return val, w


def soft_c5_value(h, n, alpha):
    p, q = compute_pq(h)
    c = np.empty(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    c[:n] = p[::-1]
    c[n:] = q[1:]
    val, _ = soft_max_weights(c, alpha)
    return val


def grad_from_weights(h, wp, wq):
    n = h.size
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n <= 1:
        return g
    wpw = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    wpw[1:] = wp[1:]
    wqw = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    wqw[1:] = wq[1:]

    cum_p = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    cum_p[1:] = np.cumsum(wpw[1:])
    tail_p = cum_p[n - 1 - np.arange(n)]
    conv1_p = fast_conv(wpw[::-1], h)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    conv2_p = fast_conv(wpw, h)[:n]
    g += tail_p - conv1_p - conv2_p

    cum_q = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    cum_q[1:] = np.cumsum(wqw[1:])
    conv1_q = fast_conv(wqw[::-1], h)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    conv2_q = fast_conv(wqw, h)[:n]
    g += cum_q - conv2_q - conv1_q

    g += wp[0] * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
    return g


def soft_c5_grad(h, n, alpha):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n <= 1:
        p = np.array([h[0] * (1.0 - h[0])])
        q = p.copy()
        val, w = soft_max_weights(p, alpha)
        g = p[0] if w[0] == 0 else (1.0 - 2.0 * h[0])
        return val, np.array([g[0] * w[0]])
    p, q = compute_pq(h)
    c = np.empty(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    c[:n] = p[::-1]
    c[n:] = q[1:]
    val, w = soft_max_weights(c, alpha)
    wp = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    wq = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    wp[0] = w[n - 1]
    wp[1:] = w[:n - 1][::-1]
    wq[1:] = w[n:]
    g = grad_from_weights(h, wp, wq)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    return float(val), g


def lbfgs_obj(x, n, alpha, mu):
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    val = soft_c5_value(x, n, alpha) * (2.0 / n)
    diff = x.sum() - (n / 2.0)
    val += 0.5 * mu * diff * diff
    return float(val)


def lbfgs_grad(x, n, alpha, mu):
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    _, g = soft_c5_grad(x, n, alpha)
    g = g * (2.0 / n)
    diff = x.sum() - (n / 2.0)
    g += mu * diff
    return g


def lbfgs_refine(h, n, deadline, alpha_list, best_h, best_c, mu=50.0):
    hcur = project_feasible(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), n / 2.0)
    target = n / 2.0
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    options = {
        "maxiter": 140,
        "ftol": 1e-18,
        "gtol": 1e-10,
        "maxfun": 2000,
        "maxls": 50,
        "maxcor": 50,
        "disp": False,
    }
    for alpha in alpha_list:
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        stale = 0
        for _ in range(3):
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    lbfgs_obj,
                    hcur,
                    jac=lbfgs_grad,
                    args=(n, alpha, mu),
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    bounds=bounds,
                    options=options,
                )
            except Exception:
                break
            hproj = project_feasible(res.x, target)
            c = compute_c5_exact(hproj, n)
            if c < best_c - 1e-13:
                best_c = c
                best_h = hproj.copy()
                stale = 0
            else:
                stale += 1
            hcur = hproj
            if stale >= 2:
                break
    return best_h, float(best_c)


def apply_flip(h, p, q, x, newval):
    old = float(h[x])
    d = float(newval) - old
    if d == 0.0:
        return
    p0_delta = d * (1.0 - old - newval)
    p[0] += p0_delta
    q[0] += p0_delta
    h[x] = float(newval)
    n = h.size
    if x < n - 1:
        p[1:n - x] += d * (1.0 - h[x + 1:n])
        q[1:n - x] -= d * h[x + 1:n]
    if x > 0:
        p[1:x + 1] -= d * h[x - 1::-1]
        q[1:x + 1] += d * (1.0 - h[x - 1::-1])


def apply_swap_inplace(h, p, q, i, j):
    apply_flip(h, p, q, i, 0.0)
    apply_flip(h, p, q, j, 1.0)


def apply_transfer_inplace(h, p, q, i, j, t):
    t = float(t)
    a = float(h[i])
    b = float(h[j])
    t = min(t, a, 1.0 - b)
    if t <= 1e-14:
        return
    apply_flip(h, p, q, i, a - t)
    apply_flip(h, p, q, j, b + t)


def swap_new_max(h, p, q, i, j):
    ht = h.copy()
    pt = p.copy()
    qt = q.copy()
    apply_swap_inplace(ht, pt, qt, i, j)
    return float(max(pt.max(), qt.max()))


def transfer_new_max(h, p, q, i, j, t):
    ht = h.copy()
    pt = p.copy()
    qt = q.copy()
    a = float(ht[i])
    b = float(ht[j])
    t = min(float(t), a, 1.0 - b)
    if t <= 1e-14:
        return float(max(pt.max(), qt.max()))
    apply_flip(ht, pt, qt, i, a - t)
    apply_flip(ht, pt, qt, j, b + t)
    return float(max(pt.max(), qt.max()))


def zero_counts(h, n):
    ones = (h > 0.5).astype(float)
    S = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(ones)))
    idx = np.arange(n)
    ZR = (n - 1 - idx) - (S[-1] - S[1:])
    ZB = idx - S[:-1]
    return ZR, ZB


def active_gradient(typ, r, h, n):
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if typ == 0:
        g[:] = 1.0 - 2.0 * h
        return g
    m = n - int(r)
    if m <= 0:
        return g
    if typ == 1:
        g[0:m] += 1.0 - h[int(r):]
        g[int(r):] -= h[0:m]
    else:
        g[int(r):] += 1.0 - h[0:m]
        g[0:m] -= h[int(r):]
    return g


def gradient_pair_improve(h, p, q, n, alpha, deadline):
    _, g = soft_c5_grad(h, n, alpha)
    pos = np.where(g > 1e-7)[0]
    neg = np.where(g < -1e-7)[0]
    if pos.size == 0 or neg.size == 0:
        return False
    pos = pos[np.argsort(-g[pos])[:15]]
    neg = neg[np.argsort(g[neg])[:15]]
    cur = float(max(p.max(), q.max()))
    best_new = cur
    best_pair = None
    evals = 0
    fracs = (0.02, 0.08, 0.2, 0.45, 0.8, 1.0)
    for i in pos:
        ii = int(i)
        hi = float(h[ii])
        if hi <= 1e-9:
            continue
        for j in neg:
            jj = int(j)
            hj = float(h[jj])
            if hj >= 1.0 - 1e-9 or ii == jj:
                continue
            lim = min(hi, 1.0 - hj)
            if lim <= 1e-9:
                continue
            for frac in fracs:
                t = lim * frac
                nm = transfer_new_max(h, p, q, ii, jj, t)
                evals += 1
                if nm < best_new - 1e-12:
                    best_new = nm
                    best_pair = (ii, jj, t)
                if evals > 900 or time.time() > deadline:
                    break
            if time.time() > deadline or evals > 900:
                break
        if time.time() > deadline or evals > 900:
            break
    if best_pair is None:
        return False
    apply_transfer_inplace(h, p, q, best_pair[0], best_pair[1], best_pair[2])
    p_new, q_new = compute_pq(h)
    p[:] = p_new
    q[:] = q_new
    new_cur = float(max(p.max(), q.max()))
    return new_cur < cur - 1e-12


def fractional_active_descent(h, n, deadline):
    target = n / 2.0
    h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), target)
    p, q = compute_pq(h)
    cur = float(max(p.max(), q.max()))
    best_c = cur
    best_h = h.copy()

    for _ in range(800):
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        cmax = cur
        tol = max(1e-9, 1e-6 * cmax)
        types = []
        rs = []
        vals = []
        if p[0] >= cmax - tol:
            types.append(0)
            rs.append(0)
            vals.append(float(p[0]))
        for r in range(1, n):
            if p[r] >= cmax - tol:
                types.append(1)
                rs.append(r)
                vals.append(float(p[r]))
            if q[r] >= cmax - tol:
                types.append(2)
                rs.append(r)
                vals.append(float(q[r]))
        best_new = cur
        best_pair = None
        if types:
            order = np.argsort(vals)[::-1][:10]
            for oi in order:
                typ = int(types[oi])
                r = int(rs[oi])
                g = active_gradient(typ, r, h, n)
                pos = np.where(g > 1e-8)[0]
                neg = np.where(g < -1e-8)[0]
                if pos.size == 0 or neg.size == 0:
                    continue
                pos = pos[np.argsort(-g[pos])[:12]]
                neg = neg[np.argsort(g[neg])[:12]]
                fracs = (0.01, 0.04, 0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0)
                for i in pos:
                    ii = int(i)
                    hi = float(h[ii])
                    if hi <= 1e-9:
                        continue
                    for j in neg:
                        jj = int(j)
                        hj = float(h[jj])
                        if hj >= 1.0 - 1e-9 or ii == jj:
                            continue
                        lim = min(hi, 1.0 - hj)
                        if lim <= 1e-9:
                            continue
                        for frac in fracs:
                            t = lim * frac
                            nm = transfer_new_max(h, p, q, ii, jj, t)
                            if nm < best_new - 1e-12:
                                best_new = nm
                                best_pair = (ii, jj, t)
                        if time.time() >= deadline:
                            break
                    if time.time() >= deadline:
                        break
                if time.time() >= deadline:
                    break

        if best_pair is None:
            improved = gradient_pair_improve(h, p, q, n, 50000.0, deadline)
            if not improved:
                break
        else:
            apply_transfer_inplace(h, p, q, best_pair[0], best_pair[1], best_pair[2])

        p, q = compute_pq(h)
        cur = float(max(p.max(), q.max()))
        if cur < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = cur
            best_h = h.copy()

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n / 2.0)
    return best_h, compute_c5_exact(best_h, n)


def binary_local_search(h0, n, deadline, rng):
    target = n // 2
    h = binarize(h0, float(target))
    p, q = compute_pq(h)
    cur = float(max(p.max(), q.max()))
    best_c = cur
    best_h = h.copy()
    ones = list(np.where(h > 0.5)[0])
    zeros = list(np.where(h < 0.5)[0])
    temp = 0.25
    no_improve = 0
    top_ones = list(ones[:1])
    top_zeros = list(zeros[:1])
    it = 0
    max_iters = 1000000
    score_interval = 15

    while it < max_iters and time.time() < deadline and ones and zeros:
        if it % score_interval == 0:
            maxp = float(p.max())
            maxq = float(q.max())
            ZR, ZB = zero_counts(h, n)
            ones_arr = np.array(ones, dtype=int)
            zeros_arr = np.array(zeros, dtype=int)
            if ones_arr.size == 0 or zeros_arr.size == 0:
                break
            if maxp >= maxq:
                so = ZR[ones_arr] - 0.35 * ZB[ones_arr]
                sz = ZR[zeros_arr] + 0.35 * ZB[zeros_arr]
            else:
                so = ZB[ones_arr] - 0.35 * ZR[ones_arr]
                sz = ZB[zeros_arr] + 0.35 * ZR[zeros_arr]
            top_ones = ones_arr[np.argsort(-so)[:25]].tolist()
            top_zeros = zeros_arr[np.argsort(sz)[:25]].tolist()
            if not top_ones or not top_zeros:
                break

        best_nm = cur + 1.0
        best_i = -1
        best_j = -1
        eval_count = 0
        for e in range(4):
            ii = int(top_ones[(e * 3) % len(top_ones)])
            jj = int(top_zeros[(e * 5 + 1) % len(top_zeros)])
            if h[ii] < 0.5 or h[jj] > 0.5 or ii == jj:
                continue
            nm = swap_new_max(h, p, q, ii, jj)
            eval_count += 1
            if nm < best_nm:
                best_nm = nm
                best_i = ii
                best_j = jj
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break
        if no_improve > 300 and eval_count < 20:
            for _ in range(3):
                if time.time() >= deadline:
                    break
                ii = int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
                jj = int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
                if h[ii] < 0.5 or h[jj] > 0.5 or ii == jj:
                    continue
                nm = swap_new_max(h, p, q, ii, jj)
                eval_count += 1
                if nm < best_nm:
                    best_nm = nm
                    best_i = ii
                    best_j = jj

        if best_i >= 0:
            d = best_nm - cur
            accept = (d < -1e-12) or (d > 0 and rng.random() < np.exp(-d / temp))
            if accept:
                apply_swap_inplace(h, p, q, best_i, best_j)
                try:
                    ones.remove(best_i)
                except ValueError:
                    pass
                try:
                    zeros.remove(best_j)
                except ValueError:
                    pass
                zeros.append(best_i)
                ones.append(best_j)
                cur = best_nm
                no_improve = 0
                if cur < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = cur
                    best_h = h.copy()
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        it += 1
        temp = max(0.02, temp * 0.99985)

        if no_improve > 800:
            k = int(rng.randint(3, 12))
            for _ in range(k):
                if not ones or not zeros:
                    break
                ii = int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
                jj = int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
                if h[ii] < 0.5 or h[jj] > 0.5 or ii == jj:
                    continue
                apply_swap_inplace(h, p, q, ii, jj)
                try:
                    ones.remove(ii)
                except ValueError:
                    pass
                try:
                    zeros.remove(jj)
                except ValueError:
                    pass
                zeros.append(ii)
                ones.append(jj)
            p, q = compute_pq(h)
            cur = float(max(p.max(), q.max()))
            no_improve = 0
            if cur < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = cur
                best_h = h.copy()

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n / 2.0)
    return best_h, compute_c5_exact(best_h, n)


def thue_morse(n, rng):
    score = np.array([(int(i).bit_count() & 1) for i in range(n)], dtype=float)
    score += 1e-9 * rng.random_sample(n)
    return binarize(score, n / 2.0)


def random_balanced(n, rng):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    m = n // 2
    if m <= 0:
        return h
    idx = rng.choice(n, m, replace=False)
    h[idx] = 1.0
    return h


def beatty_binary(n, alpha, offset, rng):
    m = n // 2
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if m <= 0:
        return h
    base = (np.floor(np.arange(m) * float(alpha) + float(offset)).astype(int)) % n
    uniq = np.unique(base)
    h[uniq] = 1.0
    if uniq.size < m:
        zeros = np.where(h == 0)[0]
        if zeros.size:
            add = rng.choice(zeros, size=min(m - uniq.size, zeros.size), replace=False)
            h[add] = 1.0
    if h.sum() > m:
        ones = np.where(h == 1)[0]
        rm = int(h.sum() - m)
        if ones.size >= rm:
            h[ones[:rm]] = 0.0
    return h


def wave_binary(n, freq, phase, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    score = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(freq) * x + float(phase))
    score += 0.25 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    return binarize(score, n / 2.0)


def make_starts(n, parent, rng):
    starts = []
    if parent is not None and len(parent) == n:
        starts.append(project_feasible(parent, n / 2.0))
        starts.append(binarize(parent, n / 2.0))
    starts.append(thue_morse(n, rng))
    starts.append(random_balanced(n, rng))
    starts.append(random_balanced(n, rng))
    alphas = [0.6180339887, 0.7071067812, 0.6666666667, 0.5555555556]
    for a in alphas:
        starts.append(beatty_binary(n, a, float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0)), rng))
    starts.append(wave_binary(n, 1.0, 0.0, rng))
    starts.append(wave_binary(n, 2.0, 1.0, rng))
    starts.append(wave_binary(n, 3.0, 2.0, rng))
    starts.append(wave_binary(n, 5.0, 0.5, rng))
    return starts


def fourier_features(n, K):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    cols = [np.ones(n, dtype=float)]
    for k in range(1, K + 1):
        ang = np.pi * float(k) * x
        cols.append(np.cos(ang))
        cols.append(np.sin(ang))
    return np.column_stack(cols)


def fourier_h(c, X):
    f = X.dot(c)
    f = np.clip(f, -50.0, 50.0)
    return 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-f))


def fourier_obj(c, X, n, alpha, mu):
    h = fourier_h(c, X)
    val, _ = soft_c5_grad(h, n, alpha)
    val = val * (2.0 / n)
    diff = h.sum() - (n / 2.0)
    val += 0.5 * mu * diff * diff
    return float(val)


def fourier_grad(c, X, n, alpha, mu):
    h = fourier_h(c, X)
    _, g = soft_c5_grad(h, n, alpha)
    s = h * (1.0 - h)
    diff = h.sum() - (n / 2.0)
    grad = (2.0 / n) * (X.T.dot(g * s)) + mu * diff * (X.T.dot(s))
    return grad


def fourier_refine(n, deadline, rng, h_seed):
    K = 4
    X = fourier_features(n, K)
    L = X.shape[1]
    bounds = [(-8.0, 8.0)] * L
    options = {
        "maxiter": 180,
        "ftol": 1e-16,
        "gtol": 1e-8,
        "maxfun": 2500,
        "maxls": 40,
        "maxcor": 40,
        "disp": False,
    }
    cands = []
    if h_seed is not None and len(h_seed) == n:
        try:
            cands.append(np.linalg.lstsq(X, h_seed, rcond=None)[0])
        except Exception:
            pass
    cands.append(np.zeros(L, dtype=float))
    for _ in range(3):
        cands.append(rng.standard_normal(L) * 1.5)
    cands.append(rng.choice([-3.0, 3.0], size=L) * 0.5)
    cands[0][0] = 0.0

    best_h = None
    best_c = float("inf")
    for c0 in cands:
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        c0 = np.clip(np.asarray(c0, dtype=float), -8.0, 8.0)
        for alpha in [3000.0, 20000.0]:
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    fourier_obj,
                    c0,
                    jac=fourier_grad,
                    args=(X, n, alpha, 25.0),
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    bounds=bounds,
                    options=options,
                )
                c = np.clip(res.x, -8.0, 8.0)
            except Exception:
                c = c0
            h = project_feasible(fourier_h(c, X), n / 2.0)
            cval = compute_c5_exact(h, n)
            if cval < best_c:
                best_c = cval
                best_h = h.copy()
    if best_h is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    return best_h, best_c


def softmax(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    y = y - y.max()
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        e = np.ones_like(y)
        s = e.size
    return e / s


def basis_obj(y, B, n, alpha):
    a = softmax(y)
    h = B.dot(a)
    val, _ = soft_c5_grad(h, n, alpha)
    return float(val * (2.0 / n))


def basis_grad(y, B, n, alpha):
    a = softmax(y)
    h = B.dot(a)
    _, g = soft_c5_grad(h, n, alpha)
    proj = B.T.dot(g)
    hproj = float(np.dot(g, h))
    return (2.0 / n) * (a * (proj - hproj))


def basis_refine(cands, n, deadline, best_h, best_c, rng):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    B_list = []
    for cc in cands:
        if cc is None:
            continue
        vv = np.asarray(cc, dtype=float)
        if vv.size != n:
            continue
        vv = project_feasible(vv, n / 2.0)
        dup = False
        for uu in B_list:
            if np.max(np.abs(vv - uu)) < 1e-5:
                dup = True
                break
        if not dup:
            B_list.append(vv)
            if len(B_list) >= 12:
                break
    if not B_list:
        return best_h, float(best_c)
    B = np.array(B_list, dtype=float)
    K = B.shape[0]

    starts = [np.zeros(K, dtype=float)]
    for kk in range(min(K, 5)):
        y = np.full(K, -8.0, dtype=float)
        y[kk] = 8.0
        starts.append(y)
    for _ in range(3):
        starts.append(rng.standard_normal(K) * 2.0)

    options = {
        "maxiter": 200,
        "ftol": 1e-18,
        "gtol": 1e-10,
        "maxfun": 3000,
        "maxls": 40,
        "maxcor": 40,
        "disp": False,
    }

    for y0 in starts:
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(
                basis_obj,
                y0,
                jac=basis_grad,
                args=(B, n, 20000.0),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options=options,
            )
            yres = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
        except Exception:
            yres = np.asarray(y0, dtype=float)
        a = softmax(yres)
        h = project_feasible(B.dot(a), n / 2.0)
        cur = compute_c5_exact(h, n)
        if cur < best_c:
            best_c = cur
            best_h = h.copy()

        alpha = a.copy()
        hcur = h.copy()
        for _ in range(70):
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break
            improved = False
            for s in range(K):
                if improved:
                    break
                if alpha[s] <= 1e-9:
                    continue
                for t in range(K):
                    if improved:
                        break
                    if s == t:
                        continue
                    for frac in (0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
                        delta = alpha[s] * frac
                        a2 = alpha.copy()
                        a2[s] -= delta
                        a2[t] += delta
                        if a2[s] < -1e-12 or a2[t] < -1e-12:
                            continue
                        a2 = np.clip(a2, 0.0, 1.0)
                        ss = a2.sum()
                        if ss <= 0:
                            continue
                        a2 /= ss
                        h2 = B.dot(a2)
                        c2 = compute_c5_count(h2, n) * dx
                        if c2 < cur - 1e-13:
                            alpha = a2
                            hcur = h2
                            cur = c2
                            improved = True
                            break
            if not improved:
                break
            hchk = project_feasible(hcur, n / 2.0)
            cchk = compute_c5_exact(hchk, n)
            if cchk < best_c:
                best_c = cchk
                best_h = hchk.copy()

    hfin = project_feasible(hcur, n / 2.0)
    cfin = compute_c5_exact(hfin, n)
    if cfin < best_c:
        best_c = cfin
        best_h = hfin
    return best_h, float(best_c)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    if budget_s <= 35:
        try:
            h_parent = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
            n_parent = len(h_parent)
            if n_parent <= 0:
                h_parent = np.full(500, 0.5)
                n_parent = 500
        except Exception:
            h_parent = np.full(500, 0.5)
            n_parent = 500
        h_out = project_feasible(h_parent, n_parent / 2.0)
        c_out = compute_c5_exact(h_out, n_parent)
        return h_out, float(c_out), int(n_parent)

    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 18.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    random.seed(seed)

    try:
        h_parent = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n_parent = int(h_parent.size)
    except Exception:
        h_parent = None
        n_parent = 0

    if 100 <= n_parent <= 1200 and n_parent % 2 == 0 and h_parent is not None:
        n_use = n_parent
        h_init = project_feasible(h_parent, n_use / 2.0)
    else:
        n_use = 500
        if h_parent is not None and n_parent > 0:
            h_init = resample_h(h_parent, n_use)
        else:
            h_init = np.full(n_use, 0.5, dtype=float)
        h_init = project_feasible(h_init, n_use / 2.0)

    best_h = h_init.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5_exact(best_h, n_use)

    span = max(1.0, budget_s - 18.0)
    d1 = t0 + 0.055 * span
    d2 = t0 + 0.12 * span
    d3 = t0 + 0.28 * span
    d4 = t0 + 0.56 * span
    d5 = t0 + 0.68 * span
    d6 = t0 + 0.82 * span
    d7 = t0 + 0.955 * span

    if time.time() < d1:
        h_new, c_new = fractional_active_descent(best_h, n_use, d1)
        if c_new < best_c:
            best_c = c_new
            best_h = h_new

    if time.time() < d2:
        h_f, c_f = fourier_refine(n_use, d2, rng, best_h)
        if h_f is not None and c_f < best_c:
            best_c = c_f
            best_h = h_f

    if time.time() < d3:
        h_l, c_l = lbfgs_refine(
            best_h, n_use, min(d3, time.time() + max(20.0, 0.12 * span)),
            [2000.0, 10000.0, 50000.0], best_h, best_c, 50.0
        )
        if c_l < best_c:
            best_c = c_l
            best_h = h_l
        if time.time() < d3:
            h_l2, c_l2 = lbfgs_refine(
                h_init, n_use, d3, [20000.0, 100000.0],
                h_init, compute_c5_exact(h_init, n_use), 50.0
            )
            if c_l2 < best_c:
                best_c = c_l2
                best_h = h_l2

    if time.time() < d4 and n_use % 2 == 0:
        starts = make_starts(n_use, h_init, rng)
        if len(best_h) == n_use:
            starts.insert(0, best_h)
        scored = []
        cvals = []
        for hh in starts:
            if hh is None or len(hh) != n_use:
                continue
            hh = project_feasible(hh, n_use / 2.0)
            cval = compute_c5_exact(hh, n_use)
            scored.append(hh)
            cvals.append(cval)
        if scored:
            order = np.argsort(cvals)
            top = []
            for idx in order[:4]:
                top.append(scored[int(idx)])
            if top:
                left = max(5.0, d4 - time.time())
                per = left / float(len(top))
                for idx, hs in enumerate(top):
                    phase_deadline = min(d4, time.time() + per)
                    if time.time() >= phase_deadline:
                        continue
                    hb, cb = binary_local_search(hs, n_use, phase_deadline, rng)
                    if cb < best_c:
                        best_c = cb
                        best_h = hb
                    if time.time() < d4 and cb <= best_c + 1e-12:
                        ha, ca = fractional_active_descent(hb, n_use, d4)
                        if ca < best_c:
                            best_c = ca
                            best_h = ha

    if time.time() < d5:
        basis_cands = []
        if best_h is not None:
            basis_cands.append(best_h)
        basis_cands.append(h_init)
        if h_parent is not None and len(h_parent) == n_use:
            basis_cands.append(h_parent)
        extra = make_starts(n_use, h_init, rng)
        basis_cands.extend(extra[:6])
        hb, cb = basis_refine(basis_cands, n_use, d5, best_h, best_c, rng)
        if cb < best_c:
            best_c = cb
            best_h = hb

    if time.time() < d6:
        h_l, c_l = lbfgs_refine(
            best_h, n_use, d6,
            [3000.0, 20000.0, 100000.0],
            best_h, best_c, 50.0
        )
        if c_l < best_c:
            best_c = c_l
            best_h = h_l

    if time.time() < d7:
        ha, ca = fractional_active_descent(best_h, n_use, d7)
        if ca < best_c:
            best_c = ca
            best_h = ha

    if time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
        ha, ca = fractional_active_descent(best_h, n_use, deadline - 2.0)
        if ca < best_c:
            best_c = ca
            best_h = ha

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n_use / 2.0)
    best_c = compute_c5_exact(best_h, n_use)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(n_use)
```
